## Scope and self-hash rule

- Physical files represented: **691**
- Total bytes represented: **125291422**
- Exclusions: `.git` internals, caches, editor locks, temporary files, OS trash and the `/tmp` task staging directory.
- `FILE_CATALOG.md` is listed with `self-hash controlled externally by documentation release manifest`; recursive self-hash embedding is not attempted.
- `REPOSITORY_DOCUMENTATION_RELEASE_MANIFEST.md` is listed with its final byte size but its hash is controlled in the terminal release report because the manifest records the catalog hash, creating a mutual hash dependency.
- Classifications are conservative. Exact authority disputes are governed by the authority map and controlling source content.

## Interpretation boundaries

- Exact hash equality proves byte identity only; it does not create an independent source or vote.
- `UNCLASSIFIED / OWNER REVIEW REQUIRED` is deliberate where content/provenance does not support a confident role.
- Historical status wording remains historically valid even when current controls have moved on.
- Failed/concept-swapped material is preserved for traceability and must not be promoted.
- Master Manual drafting/review work products are not the final controlled release.
- The frozen Master Manual v1.0 and Decision Register v1.2 remain unchanged by this catalog.
